## What to check, per defect

1. **Driver disconnected.** Stage order (merge+audit → unfold → endpoint-evidence); the
   validator and projector invocations must use only options those scripts define and must
   supply every `required=True` one; the candidate ROOT key must be one the builder writes.
   Confirm `hCov_std_final5_candidate` is referenced by no executable line. Check
   `AGENT_A_HANDOFF.md` matches the executable contract. **Also check the STOP_AFTER default
   changed appropriately** — reordering put a receipt-WRITING stage before evidence.
2. **Resume/completion.** Does the skip path validate content, or still accept any ROOT plus
   any nonempty `.done`? Are receipt-write failures propagated? Are extra endpoint tags
   rejected? Do legacy-attested receipts now carry merged/central provenance?
3. **Manifest consistency.** Is the config fully hash-bound? Are source blobs taken from the
   COMMITTED object rather than the working tree, and does a dirty source fail closed? Is the
   recorded commit the one that introduced the recorded blob? Are `(band,index)` identities and
   BOTH migration directions enforced — including the previously dead `ZERO_SEL`?
4. **Component/adoption provenance.** Are all retained pure components persisted? Is the
   candidate published before a manifest-last write that binds its sha256? Does the validator
   consume the component manifest and recompute the full-total identity? Is the candidate-path
   guard resolved containment rather than a substring test — try to defeat it.
5. **Projection geometry.** Are edge hash, bin-volume hash and the 4D mask/order hash all
   mandatory and compared? Is an M-content hash recorded?
6. **Test coverage.** Does anything execute a real gate end to end? Is the old false-positive
   (`test_project_rejects_protected_out_path` passing `--proj`) fixed so it asserts the
   specific gate reached? Are fixtures built from the real producer rather than hand-assembled
   to match the consumer? **Run the suites yourself** —
   `python3 -m pytest nd-unfolding/tests/test_p4_repair.py nd-unfolding/tests/test_p4_resume_integration.py`
   — and report the count.

| BEN-025 | Small-sample spread estimates must not overturn decisions: a 16-seed measurement reading "sd GREW 56%" (0.7477%→1.1703%) inverted an advisor's ranking, discarded its correct Rank 1, and produced a tolerance-RAISE recommendation the user approved — but F=2.4498 on df(15,15) gives **p=0.093** and a 95% CI for the sd ratio of **[0.925, 2.648]**, containing both 1.0 and the eventual 48-seed answer 1.036. The advisor's discarded assumption (sd≈0.81%) was right to **4.2%**. Three compounding causes: a point estimate reported as a fact; the design was **paired** (shared seeds) so the two-sample F-test was the wrong instrument *and* conservative the wrong way; and the assumption was discarded for *differing* from the data rather than being *excluded* by it. Second-order: the standing "route decisions through a different account first" rule was applied to the first data cut but **not** re-applied after the data materially changed — a second opinion on superseded numbers is not a second opinion. What actually decided it was realized exceedance (6/48 vs 0/48, Fisher exact p≈0.026), not either side's gaussian tail; the closed-form window admits 0.05 at both niter=2 and 3 and cannot discriminate. Enabling vacuity: the test whose job was to prove the tolerance had power hardcoded the recoil-era operating point (R,acc,k=1.135,0.621,2) and bounded by the defect signal `(R-1)/R` instead of the binding ceiling `C=(R-1)/(2R)` — passing forever while guarding nothing, since any tol≥C is inert. Detail: `FINDING-20260806-campaign-pin-inverted-on-insignificant-variance.md`. | — | EP-2026-08-05-gate4-reissue |
| BEN-029 | Converting a launcher to the BEN-023 content-validated guard has two traps the size-only test does not catch, both hit while repairing the last three offenders on 2026-08-06. (1) **`VAR=x func` leaks in bash.** An env-assignment prefix is correctly scoped when the target is an external command, but for a shell FUNCTION bash leaves the variable set in the caller afterwards — so rewriting `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=$gpu bash "$PAYLOAD"` as `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=$gpu rg_run ... bash "$PAYLOAD"` silently pins every later loop iteration to the first GPU. Wrap the call in a subshell. (2) **A guard with no stamp is a silent allocation burn**: `rg_skip_if_complete` without a matching `rg_run`/`rg_mark_complete` for the *same output token* never skips, so every run redoes everything — safe direction, still a bug, and `test_every_guarded_output_has_a_producer_stamp` is what catches it. Prefer `rg_run` (binds produce+stamp into one call) over a bare `rg_mark_complete` placed later. Also: naming `rg_*` functions in a comment ABOVE the `source` line trips `test_every_rg_caller_sources_the_library_first`, which reads the first mention as the first use — describe the helper instead of naming it, or name `lib/resume_guard.sh` on the same line. | — | EP-2026-08-06-ben023-final-three |
| BEN-031 | **Never `git stash -u` on the shared cluster tree.** Used reflexively on 2026-08-06 to clear the way for a fast-forward merge, it swept up **2147 untracked files** — including the 302 `2d-unfolding/uq/*.root.done` completion markers that the BEN-023 resume guards read (`rg_marker_path()` is literally `printf '%s.done\n'`). With the markers gone, `rg_is_complete` returns false and a re-run of the 2D bootstrap launchers would have silently recomputed hundreds of unfolds — the exact recompute the BEN-023 conversion exists to prevent. Nothing was lost (verified all 2147 paths restored before dropping the stash) but the blast radius was large and the command was chosen without thinking about scope. Two rules: (1) on a tree another session shares, make merges succeed by moving the *specific* blocking paths aside, never by a bulk stash; (2) the untracked set in this repo is **load-bearing state**, not clutter — sentinels, markers and configs live there. Corollary to BEN-023 and to the pull-abort trap: `git` verbs whose scope is "everything not tracked" are unsafe here by default. | — | EP-2026-08-06-shared-tree |
| BEN-032 | **A filename-substring filter over a set defined by its DIRECTORIES silently under-protects, and reports the shortfall as success.** Step 0 of `PLAN-20260806-niter3-budget-and-J28-reroll.md` protects the throw slabs the J28 re-roll consumes. Selecting them with `"slab" in filename` found **365 of 542** files and printed "365 readable, 0 unreadable" — which reads as complete, because the count of what was *checked* is not the count of what *exists*. The miss was the entire **block** ensemble: those files are named `block5d_flux_17.npz`, `blockfps_*.npz`, `block4d_0.npz`, with no "slab" in the filename; only their directory (`block_slabs_5d_sb/`) says what they are. `rescale_flux_universes.py` rebuilds `C_blocksum` from exactly those, so a purge would have left Step 1 unrunnable while the manifest asserted the inputs were safe. **Two rules.** (1) When a set is defined by where its members live, select on the directory, not the filename — and state the selection criterion *inside* the artifact, so a reader can audit coverage instead of trusting a count. (2) A completeness claim needs a denominator from an independent source: `find -name '*slab*'` vs `find -path '*slab*'` differ by 49%, and nothing inside the first result set could reveal that. Corollary to the powerless-test pattern (`AUDIT-FINDINGS-20260729-B` §4) at the level of *inputs* rather than assertions: a check that runs cleanly over the wrong population is as quiet as one that asserts nothing. It also surfaced that the plan's own "365 slabs" precondition came from the same filter, so the plan inherited the gap it was written to close. | — | EP-2026-08-06-niter3-budget |
| BEN-033 | **Read an ensemble's size from the PRODUCT, never from the launcher that built it — a launcher's globs say what it *would* consume, the product records what it *did*.** On 2026-08-06 I identified the inputs to the J28 re-roll from `sbatch_uthrow_combine_5d_fast.sh:16-19` plus a STATUS run-F entry, cross-checked that two independent sources agreed, and wrote "the ensemble the **adopted** `unified_throw_cov_5d.root` was built from" into a finding, the ledger, the RUN_LOG and a STATUS one-liner. The adopted ROOT itself says `n_throws = 160`; the re-roll processed **122**. `uthrow_slabs_5d_sb/` holds slabs 0–30 and slabs **31–39 are gone** (~38 throws) — lost from purgeable scratch after the combine ran. Two agreeing *sources* were still both the wrong *kind* of source, so agreement bought nothing; one `TFile.Open` + `Get("n_throws")` would have caught it in seconds. Consequences: the corrected absolute numbers are a 76.2% subsample and not drop-in replacements for the adopted covariance (the "before" sits −2.62% below the adopted `sqrt_tr_unified`), though the before→after *relative* changes remain controlled because both sides use the same slabs. **Rules.** (1) When a receipt or ROOT records `n_throws`/`n_replicas`/`expected_*`, read it and compare against what the glob actually resolves *today* — the number in the product is the only statement about the past. (2) `--expected-throws 0-159` resolving to 122 files is a **failed precondition**, not a detail; a re-roll tool should refuse or loudly label a run whose input count disagrees with the product it is correcting. (3) Corollary to BEN-032: that finding was about a filter under-selecting *existing* files, this one is about files that no longer exist — a protection step (Step 0) can only ever protect the survivors, so it cannot substitute for checking what was lost. | — | EP-2026-08-06-niter3-budget |
| BEN-034 | **`ControlPersist` masks NERSC certificate expiry, so cluster access dies silently mid-session hours after the credential lapsed — and the only outbound comms path dies with it.** `~/.ssh/nersc-cert.pub` is an sshproxy certificate valid `2026-08-05T06:07` → `2026-08-06T06:08:08` (~24 h). `~/.ssh/config` sets `ControlMaster auto` / `ControlPath ~/.ssh/cm/%r@%h:%p` / `ControlPersist 12h`, so every `ssh` after expiry kept succeeding over the *already-authenticated* master socket. | BEN-035 | **`rc=$?` after a pipeline reports the LAST stage's status, so a diagnostic that pipes its command through `tail`/`grep` cannot detect that command failing.** Hit twice on 2026-08-06. First: an `srun` suite launch whose task-count check keyed on a banner the same command had suppressed, so the check could not fire. Second: a three-host ssh reachability probe written as `ssh … | tail -2` then `rc=$?` — it printed `ssh_rc=0` for three hosts that had all in fact hung, because `tail` exited 0 on empty input. Both cost a full diagnostic round trip and, worse, briefly asserted the opposite of the truth. **Rules.** (1) Capture the status of the command you are testing with no pipe in between (`cmd > f 2>&1; rc=$?`), then filter *reads* of `f` — the same shape BEN-026 prescribes for run logs, for the same reason. (2) `set -o pipefail` fixes the exit code but not the reasoning; prefer separating "run and record" from "filter and judge". (3) Corroborate a zero exit with evidence of *work done* (bytes produced, artifact present), because a silent success and a silent hang look identical through a pipe. Sibling trap in the same family: `grep` block-buffers when its stdout is a pipe or file, so a `ssh -v … \| grep … > f` diagnostic shows an EMPTY file while the connection is stalling — redirect the whole stream, then grep the file. **Third instance, 2026-08-07, and the sharpest:** `[ "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$(git rev-parse origin/main)" ] && echo PUSH CONFIRMED` printed CONFIRMED while the push had been **rejected** and the commit sat unapplied in a *conflicted rebase* — because during a conflicted rebase `HEAD` is checked out at the upstream, so the two hashes are equal precisely in the failure case. The check was not merely uninformative, it was **anti-correlated** with success. A confirmation must be able to distinguish success from the specific failure it is meant to catch: the working form asserts three things — no `.git/rebase-merge`/`rebase-apply`, the commit's subject is reachable from `origin/main`, and `HEAD == origin/main`. General rule for this family: before trusting a check, ask what it prints when the thing fails. **Fourth instance, 2026-08-07, and it went the other way: I inferred a SERVICE OUTAGE from one client command failing twice.** `squeue -u josephrb` returned `Unable to contact slurm controller` at 09:19Z and again at 09:49Z, and I reported a sustained Slurm outage. There was none: `scontrol ping` said `Slurmctld(primary) is UP`, `sinfo` listed partitions up, a single-job `squeue -j` worked, the wakerctl watch's `unreliable` counter was still **0** (so the tick's own `sacct` calls had all succeeded), and three immediate retries of the user-wide query succeeded 3/3. The *user-wide* query is simply heavier than a single-job one and times out intermittently under load. Note both directions of this error occurred within hours: earlier the same day an empty `squeue` with stderr discarded led me to report that every job had vanished. **Rule: never infer service state from one client command. Probe the service independently — `scontrol ping`, `sinfo`, a single-job query — and check any local state that would corroborate (here the watch's `unreliable` counter was decisive, because it records whether something else had been reaching Slurm all along).** | — | EP-2026-08-06-niter3-budget |

| BEN-028 | **A quiet log does not mean a dead job: on this Lustre filesystem Python block-buffers redirected stdout at 4 MiB.** `stat -c %o` on `/pscratch` returns `4194304`, and CPython sizes its buffered writer as `max(st_blksize, DEFAULT_BUFFER_SIZE)` — so `python3 script.py > out.log 2>&1` emits **nothing** on stdout until the process exits, while unbuffered stderr (TF init, warnings) appears immediately. The signature in every completed run here is identical: all stderr first, then one contiguous block of stdout at the end. On 2026-08-05 job `56375160` showed 7789 bytes and zero `ITERATION:` lines after 70 minutes and read as stalled; it was exactly half done and finished on time with VERDICT PASS. This is the same trap as the `salloc` lesson in `AGENTS.md` (a healthy q3 sweep was once **cancelled** because its log looked stuck), recurring through a different mechanism, which is why it needs its own entry. Correct liveness tests, in order: `sstat -j <id> --format=AveCPU` (CPU time accruing faster than wall time = working), produced artifacts, and any per-run stderr marker with a known rate. Do not judge by log growth. Fix at the source with `python3 -u` or `PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1` in launchers whose progress you intend to watch. | — | EP-2026-08-05-gate4-reissue |

| BEN-038 | **An L1 criterion turns per-cell VARIANCE into what reads as bias, and a predeclared rule phrased on the aggregate will confirm the wrong cause.** The D2 powered closure scores `1 - E_w[|1-r_b|]` over 285 cells. Because of the absolute value, symmetric per-cell noise is a one-sided penalty, so an unbiased-but-noisy estimator and a biased one produce the same aggregate. Decomposed on the baseline: signed mean response `E_w[r] = 0.63129` against a dilution ideal of `0.63321` — **bias −0.0019** — while the scatter penalty `E_w[|1-r|] - |1-E_w[r]| = 0.08443` accounts for **97.8%** of the whole 0.086354 gap to the ceiling, with 87 of 262 occupied bins OVERSHOOTING. `OPEN_ITEMS.md` (a) reached the scatter diagnosis first; the reusable traps are downstream of it. **Three of them, all hit in one session.** (1) Reading the per-band L1 column as undershoot: the `a_b >= 0.50` band reads 0.7943 against a 0.9704 ceiling and looks like a 17.6 pp deficit, but its signed bias is **−0.0116** and the top band actually overshoots (`E_w[r] = 1.0333`) — the L1 gap there is entirely scatter. This session wrote the wrong reading into a draft finding before the signed split caught it. (2) A predeclared decision rule stated on the aggregate: unit-testing it with a synthetic pure-bias-shift arm returned CONFIRMED for under-fitting, because a bias shift and a scatter reduction move the aggregate identically. Fixed by making the scatter term the primary target and adding a distinct `AGGREGATE_MOVED_BUT_NOT_VIA_SCATTER` verdict. (3) Diagnosing "where is the residual" by a per-cell LABEL correlated with the mechanism: `i_pparallel = 0` was retracted as an artifact, its replacement (tilt direction) is confounded with acceptance, and only stratifying by the quantity the mechanism depends on, each stratum against **its own** ceiling, is stable. **Rule.** For any absolute-value or chi-square-like figure of merit over many cells, split it into signed response and scatter BEFORE diagnosing, and predeclare on the term you actually intend to move. | CLM-010 (i) | EP-2026-08-07-d2-underfitting |
| BEN-042 | **A decomposition's "scatter penalty" is not what removing the bias would cost, and quoting it against the criterion's headroom inverts the answer twice.** Diagnosing D2 I found that `FINDING-20260806-niter4-decision.md` 2b compares its scatter penalty `E_w[|1-r|] - |1-E_w[r]| = 0.08443` — weight-NORMALISED, a fraction of the gap — against `residual_budget_abs = 0.046854`, an ABSOLUTE L1 (`0.20 x gap`). In consistent units the penalty is 2.37x *inside* the headroom, so the published "exceeds" is a unit mismatch and the conclusion it supports looks refuted. **It is not, and stopping there is the trap.** With `x_b = 1 - r_b`, the cost of a perfect-mean-response estimator is `E_w[|x - E_w[x]|]` (a weighted MAD), NOT `E_w[|x|] - |E_w[x]|`: the two differ because `|.|` is nonlinear under a shift — if every `x_b` shared a sign the penalty would be 0 while the spread was untouched. Measured, MAD/penalty = **4.38**, the honest counterfactual is recovery **0.6302 (FAIL)**, and the dispersion EXCEEDS the headroom by 1.85x. So the conclusion holds by a route its own arithmetic did not take, and I had a written "2b is refuted on a unit error" before computing the counterfactual. **Second consequence, same root:** the penalty is a poor dispersion proxy, so a budget ladder "read on the scatter axis" through it reports a 68.3% collapse ep8->ep16 where the MAD moved 5.5% — the penalty tracks how many cells straddle `r = 1`, and 2x budget pushed 38 of 86 overshooting cells back under. **Rules.** (1) A counterfactual on a non-linear figure of merit must be RECOMPUTED under the counterfactual, never read off an additive decomposition term. (2) Before comparing two quantities from different documents, check both are normalised the same way — `_abs` in one name and a weighted mean in the other is the tell. (3) Sibling of BEN-038: that entry prescribes splitting into signed response and scatter before diagnosing, and this one is what the split's second term does and does not mean. (4) When a correction makes a load-bearing conclusion look refuted, finish the calculation before writing the refutation — the reference point, not the conclusion, was wrong. | CLM-010 (i) | EP-2026-08-07-d2-underfitting |
| BEN-044 | **A runbook can describe a gate as a checkpoint to pass when it is actually an open BLOCK with enumerated, never-repaired defects — and the state table it ships with will not say so.** `RUNBOOK-20260807-gbdt-closeout.md` G-4 reads "Pass the independent verifier, then build the standard lateral component", and its §1 state table lists every other precondition (coverage COMPLETE, laterals ADOPTED, J28 RESOLVED, P4-5D CANDIDATE) but has **no row for the verifier's standing verdict**. 27:| 21 | **`run_p4_standard.sh`'s "HARD GATE" on covariance construction checks only that a variable is non-empty, so it cannot distinguish a verifier PASS from a caller asserting one.** `:41` reads `if [[ -z "${P4_VERIFIER_PASS}" ]]; then … Refusing.` — any string (`P4_VERIFIER_PASS=x`) authorizes stages 4–6. Nothing binds the value to a `standard-p4-verifier` verdict, a receipt file, or the committed patch hash that the comment at `:11` says the verifier must PASS on. Found 2026-08-07 while scoping how much of the GBDT close-out could run unattended. Two reasons this matters more than it looks: (a) the refusal message *names the variable to set*, so the failure mode is not "agent guesses the bypass" but "gate documents its own bypass to whoever hits it"; (b) it guards the transition from validated inputs to a candidate publication covariance, which is exactly where an independent check is worth the most. Note the chain is otherwise well-shaped here — it stops at `std_final5_candidate.root` and prints `CANDIDATE only; adoption is a separate authorized step`, so the *adoption* boundary is real; it is only the verifier boundary that is nominal. Same family as BEN-040 (a fail-closed gate that cannot fail in the direction that matters). Fix options, cheapest first: require the token to equal the sha256 of a `standard-p4-verifier` receipt JSON whose `verdict` is PASS and whose `code_rev` matches `git rev-parse HEAD`; or drop the env var and read that receipt directly. **Until fixed, treat the gate as a human checkpoint and do not let an executing agent set the variable** — recorded as a trap in the GBDT close-out runbook §5. **2026-08-07 — THIS GATE WAS LOAD-BEARING, not hypothetical.** The GBDT close-out session was instructed to run G-0→G-4 unattended, reached this gate, and stopped without setting the variable. Had it self-authorized — which the refusal message invites by naming the variable — stages 4–6 would have run and **broken a stage**: they are non-executable at HEAD (#22), so the chain would have written a candidate ROOT at stage 4 and then died on an argparse error at stage 5, leaving an unvalidated candidate covariance on disk in the candidate namespace with no validation receipt and no projection. Worse, the failure would have looked like a transient tooling problem rather than what it is — the still-outstanding defect 1 of six that BLOCKed repair-3 (BEN-044) — so the likely next move under "finish the task" pressure is to *fix the flags* and re-run, producing a chain that executes and is still wrong on the other three sub-parts of defect 1 plus defects 2–6. **So the gate's value here was not that it caught a bad candidate; it was that it stopped an agent from turning six known unrepaired defects into a plausible-looking artifact.** That is the argument for binding the token to a receipt rather than deleting the gate as advisory: an advisory gate that is honoured only by convention did, in this instance, do the entire job. Note also that a fix must not be "check the variable harder" — an agent that can set an env var can write a receipt file; bind it to the sha256 of a `standard-p4-verifier` receipt whose `verdict` is PASS **and** whose `code_rev` equals `git rev-parse HEAD`, or drop the variable and read that receipt directly. | **OPEN — gate is advisory in practice; treat as human checkpoint. Three separable mismatches. **(a) Validator CLI:** the driver passes `--active … --support … --merged-dir …` (`run_p4_standard.sh:49-52`), but `p4_validate_active_lateral.py:35-39` defines `--candidate --support --manifest --merged-audit --out`, all `required=True`. `--active` and `--merged-dir` do not exist, and three required options are absent. **(b) Projector CLI:** the driver passes `--proj "${CAND}/M_5d_to_4d.npz"` (`run_p4_standard.sh:54`), but `p4_project_4d.py:46-49` defines only `--c5 --manifest --out --central-rel`; there is no `--proj`. **(c) Nonexistent ROOT key:** the driver names `hCov_std_final5_candidate` twice (`run_p4_standard.sh:50,53`), and a repo-wide grep finds that string **only in those two lines** — nothing writes it. `p4_build_components.py:159-162` writes `hCov_active5d_<band>`, `hCov_active5d_total`, `hCov_stdsyst5d_total_candidate`, and `hCov_stdcombined5d_total_candidate`. **Why it survived:** `STOP_AFTER` defaults to `evidence`, so the default path stops at stage 2 and stages 4–6 have never been executed — an unexecuted path is an untested one (the BEN-040 lesson, one lane over). The 2026-08-07 preflight ran cleanly precisely because it stopped before this. **Do not repair by editing the driver alone** — the argument names are the visible symptom, and verifier defect 1 also requires the stage ORDER to change (merged audit → unfold → endpoint evidence) and `AGENT_A_HANDOFF.md:95` to be updated to the same executable contract. Repairing only the flags would produce a chain that runs and is still wrong. **Confirmed independently by Joseph 2026-08-07**, who checked all three mismatches himself and authorized repair-4 as this lane's next packet. | **OPEN — stages 4–6 cannot run; defect 1 of the six-defect repair-4 scope, now authorized (BEN-044)** | **`docs/orchestration/REPAIR4-DEFECT-STATUS-20260807.md` — all six defects with per-sub-part HEAD status**; `docs/orchestration/runs/standard-p4-verifier/20260718T182040Z-send-8e4ca3d7.jsonl` (the original BLOCK, with citations); `nd-unfolding/run_p4_standard.sh:49-55` |
29:| 23 | **`p4_evidence.py` binds the code that exists NOW, not the code that produced the artifacts it is attesting — so re-running it silently re-attributes the endpoints to a newer driver and a newer C++ binary.** Found 2026-08-07 by re-running the (idempotent) evidence stage and diffing the tracked `p4_standard_manifest.json`. Every **physics** binding was stable — `central5d_sha256`, `central4d_sha256`, `mask5d_hash`/`mask4d_hash`, `config_hash`, `endpoint_sha256`, `axis_edges` all byte-identical — which is the reassuring half. The provenance half moved: `binary_sha256` `6b60fc51…` → `61d7dfbf…` (`binary_mtime` +290,733 s ≈ 3.4 days), `source_blobs.unfold` `7b65ebcf…` → `dc74c38f…`, `source_blobs.launcher` `559bc3fb…` → `f2a49e7d…`, plus the corresponding `source_commits`. The cause is structural, not a race: `:150-151` hashes `MINERvA101/opt/bin/runEventLoopOmniFold` **as it is on disk at run time**, and `_blob`/`_srccommit` (`:137-141`) hash the **working-tree** copy of each source path. Neither is tied to the artifact being described. **Why it matters:** the ten endpoint ROOTs are dated 2026-07-18 and were produced by the *older* driver blob; after a re-run the manifest asserts today's `unfold` blob and today's binary alongside 07-18 `endpoint_sha256` values, i.e. it claims a producer that demonstrably did not produce them. Nothing is corrupted and no number moves — but the manifest is weaker as evidence exactly where it is meant to be strongest, and an attestation built on it inherits the mis-attribution. This is the standing verifier's **defect 3** ("regenerate evidence only from exact committed blobs … record the commit containing each blob") observed live. **Mitigations, cheapest first:** record the producing blob/binary from the endpoint `.done` receipts rather than re-deriving it from the working tree; or fail closed when `source_commits.unfold` is not an ancestor of the commit that the receipts name; or split the manifest's "what exists now" fields from its "what produced this" fields so the two can never be read as one claim. The 2026-08-07 preflight's regeneration was reverted (`git checkout --`) so the committed manifest still records the 07-18 producers, and a copy of it is preserved at `docs/orchestration/state/p4-standard-attestation/p4_standard_manifest-20260718-preserved.json`. | **OPEN — provenance mis-attribution on re-run; no number affected; part of the six-defect repair-4 scope (BEN-044)** | `nd-unfolding/p4_evidence.py:137-141,150-151`; `docs/orchestration/followup-agent-A-standard-05.md` (defect 3) |

# Repair-4 scope — the six verifier defects and their status at HEAD (2026-08-07)

**Purpose.** Scope repair-4 from *all six* defects the `standard-p4-verifier` raised, not from the one
that was independently re-confirmed. Authorized by Joseph 2026-08-07 after he verified defect 1 himself.

**Source of the six.** The verifier's own final message, committed in this repo at
`docs/orchestration/runs/standard-p4-verifier/20260718T182040Z-send-8e4ca3d7.jsonl` (last
`agent_message`, 12,143 chars, opens `BLOCK`). Not a paraphrase from `followup-agent-A-standard-05.md`,
which is the derived brief. Every status below was re-checked against HEAD (`59f728e`) in one session
and cites the line it was read from.

**Verdict scope reminder.** The verifier also recorded *verified foundations* that still hold and must
not be re-litigated: the owner-neutral merged receipt (10/10 hashes, all live sizes/mtimes match), all
ten endpoint SHA256s equal to the manifest, endpoints finite/positive/65856-bin/10694-mask with
genuinely asymmetric pairs, migration 4700–4808 on `BeamAngleX/Y` and zero elsewhere, and MAT centering
as the required biased `1/N`. Re-verified independently 2026-08-07: 10/10 endpoint sha256 match, and
`EVIDENCE-COMPLETE` with all five cross-checks MATCH.

orthogonal concern. Two sub-parts were repaired incidentally by `d5bd5da` (2026-08-02), a
note-overclaims commit — see 2e and 4c.

## 1 — The authoritative driver is disconnected. **LIVE (4/4)**

Stages 4–6 cannot execute; this is `KNOWN_ISSUES.md` #22, and Joseph re-confirmed 1b–1d independently.

- **1a — stage order.** Driver still runs evidence *before* unfold (`run_p4_standard.sh:35` evidence,
  `:37` unfold), against the required merge → audit → unfold → endpoint-evidence sequence. **LIVE.**
- **1b — validator CLI.** Driver passes `--active … --support … --merged-dir …`
  (`run_p4_standard.sh:49-52`); validator requires `--candidate --support --manifest --merged-audit
  --out`, all `required=True` (`p4_validate_active_lateral.py:35-39`). Two passed options do not
  exist; three required ones are absent. **LIVE.**
- **1c — projector CLI.** Driver passes `--proj` (`run_p4_standard.sh:54`); projector defines only
  `--c5 --manifest --out --central-rel` (`p4_project_4d.py:46-49`). **LIVE.**
- **1d — nonexistent ROOT key.** Driver names `hCov_std_final5_candidate` (`:50`, `:53`); repo-wide
  grep finds it **only** on those two lines. Builder writes `hCov_active5d_<band>`,
  `hCov_active5d_total`, `hCov_stdsyst5d_total_candidate`, `hCov_stdcombined5d_total_candidate`
  (`p4_build_components.py:159-162`). **LIVE.** Also `AGENT_A_HANDOFF.md:95` still repeats the stale
  order and `--merged-dir` contract.

**Treat as unexercised code, not typos.** `STOP_AFTER` defaults to `evidence`, so stages 4–6 have
never run. Renaming flags would give a chain that executes and is still wrong — the order fix (1a) and
the handoff are part of the same defect.

## 2 — Resume/completion is not config- or source-validating. **LIVE (4/5)**

- **2a — skip is content-blind.** `run_p4_unfold_std.sh:45` skips on
  `[[ -s OUT && -s REC ]] && valid_root OUT`. Receipt tag, ROOT SHA, merged SHA, central SHA, config
  hash and source hashes are never read. **LIVE.**
- **2b — legacy receipts omit provenance.** The legacy-attest receipt carries no `merged_sha256`,
  `central5d_sha256`, or source hashes. G-1 added `bkg_mode` only. **LIVE.**
- **2c — receipt-write failure not propagated.** After the atomic ROOT rename the
  `printf … && mv` result is unchecked, so a worker can print `DONE` and return 0 with no valid
  receipt. **LIVE.**
- **2d — extra endpoint tags not rejected.** Inventory asserts the ten expected tags exist; it never
  rejects an eleventh. **LIVE.**
- **2e — merge helper fail-closed.** **REPAIRED** by `d5bd5da`: PIDs collected and waited
  individually, `NFAILED` aggregated, `EXPECTED=10` asserted (`run_p4_merge_audit_std.sh:26-48`, with
  the J31 rationale in-comment).

**Interaction with G-3.** 2a/2b are why stage 3 has not been run: attesting today writes ten receipts
in the incomplete legacy format, and 2a then skips them forever. Repair 2 **before** attesting.

## 3 — Manifest internally inconsistent, absorbed a dirty source. **LIVE (4/4)**

- **3a — config not fully hash-bound.** `p4_evidence.py:160` computes `config_hash`, then `:161` adds
  `full_phase_space_reported_grid` to `man["config"]`. The recorded hash does not cover the recorded
  config. **LIVE.**
- **3b — blobs hashed from the working tree, not commit objects.** `p4_evidence.py:137-141` hash
  working paths; `:150-151` hash the C++ binary as it is on disk. This is how the unrelated dirty
  OmniFold blob was absorbed in 2026-07. Re-demonstrated 2026-08-07: regenerating moved
  `binary_sha256` and two `source_blobs` while every physics binding stayed byte-identical
  (`KNOWN_ISSUES.md` #23). **LIVE.**
- **3c — source commits misattributed.** Consequence of 3b: recorded commits need not be the commit
  that introduced the recorded blob. **LIVE.**
- **3d — endpoint identity and migration under-checked.** `p4_evidence.py:153` asserts `band_meta`
  only; `idx_meta` is recorded at `:141` and **never asserted**, so `(band,index)` identity is not
  proven. `:156` enforces nonzero migration for `NONZERO_MIG`, and **`ZERO_SEL` (`:37`) is never
  referenced by any check** — a dead constant, so zero-migration is never enforced. **LIVE.**
- **3e — validator ignores the audit content.** It now *takes* `--merged-audit` and loads it
  (`p4_validate_active_lateral.py:38,51`) — a `d5bd5da` improvement — but a grep for
  `selection_migration_abs|census|tree_entries` in that file returns **0**. It reads the file and
  checks none of it. **LIVE.**

## 4 — Component and adoption provenance separable. **PARTIAL**

- **4a — not every retained pure component is persisted.** Builder writes five active bands plus three
  totals (`p4_build_components.py:159-162`); retained non-active blocks are not written. **LIVE.**
- **4b — manifest written before the candidate.** `json.dump(prov, …)` at `:150` precedes
  `ROOT.TFile.Open(a.out, "RECREATE")` at `:153`, so a manifest can exist for a candidate that was
  never completed — the inverse of the receipt-last discipline used elsewhere. **LIVE.**
- **4c — adoption no longer accepts any readable candidate.** **REPAIRED** by `d5bd5da`: requires
  `--i-understand-adoption` (`:24`), validator `result == "PASS"` (`:27`), per-input identity
  (`:32-33`), `pure_addition` (`:34`), out-aliasing refusal (`:38`), and binds
  `val["candidate_sha256"] == sha256_file(candidate)` (`:44-50`, the J32 fix).
- **4d — adoption still performs no promotion.** It prints `"gates PASS; would promote …"` and
  `sys.exit(0)` (`p4_adopt_standard.py:51-53`). No atomic allowlisted promotion exists. **LIVE**
  (harmless while adoption is out of scope, but it is not a working adoption path).
- **4e — candidate-path guard is substring-based.** `p4_lib.py:175` uses `CANDIDATE_SUBDIR in path`,
  not resolved containment, so a traversal through a path *containing* the candidate directory passes.
  **LIVE.**
- **4f — validator does not consume the component manifest** or independently prove the full-total
  identity. **LIVE.**

## 5 — Projection does not bind all mandatory geometry. **LIVE (4/4)**

- **5a — edge-hash check is optional.** `p4_project_4d.py:62` guards on `if "edge_hash" in man`, so a
  manifest without the key silently skips it. **LIVE.**
- **5b — bin-volume hash never validated.** Computed and written into the receipt (`:84`) but never
  compared to the manifest. **LIVE.**
- **5c — 4D mask hash not checked.** `:67` compares only the reported *count*
  (`int(m4.sum()) == man["mask4d_nreported"]`), not `mask4d_hash`. **LIVE.**
- **5d — no `M`-content hash.** Only `M_shape` is recorded (`:88`). **LIVE.**

Note the caveat already recorded in `RUNBOOK-20260807-gbdt-closeout.md` §7.4: `mask_order_hash` has no
pinned canonical target on the standard side, unlike FPS's `REPORTED_MASK_FINGERPRINT`. So 5c can prove
all components share *one* mask, not that it is the declared publication mask. **If the note quotes a
reported-bin count, pin the target constant first.**

## 6 — Tests lack integration coverage. **LIVE**

- **6a — nothing executes the shell drivers.** No test runs `run_p4_standard.sh` or
  `run_p4_unfold_std.sh`. G-1's `test_launcher_passes_bkg_mode_explicitly` asserts on the launcher's
  *text*, which is a contract check, not execution. **LIVE.**
- **6b — a green test that proves nothing.** `test_project_rejects_protected_out_path`
  (`tests/test_p4_repair.py:214-222`) still passes `--proj`, which no longer exists, so argparse exits
  nonzero **before the path guard runs**. The assertion `assertNotEqual(rc, 0)` passes for the wrong
  reason. This is the verifier's named false positive and it is still green today. **LIVE.** The fix
  is to assert the *specific* gate or error reached, never merely a nonzero return.
- **6c — missing matrix.** No fake producer, worker-failure aggregation, receipt resume/staleness,
  ordering, builder→validator happy path, or adoption identity test
  (`followup-agent-A-standard-04.md:57-62`). **LIVE.**

hand-assembled to match the consumer (BEN-040, and 6b is what the alternative looks like).

     3	# Bare-background (no nested srun) under one outer `srun --overlap --jobid=<holder>`.
     4	# Per endpoint: unique temp path -> content/config validation -> ATOMIC rename of the
     5	# ROOT -> write the receipt LAST (so a receipt implies a fully-published ROOT). Nominal
     6	# unfold (NO --universe), FIXED --seed 42 (MAT +/- cancels CV). Resume rules:
     7	#   * .done receipt present + ROOT valid           -> skip
     8	#   * legacy ROOT (no receipt) + sha256 == committed manifest attestation -> attest, write receipt
     9	#   * otherwise                                     -> (re)run transactionally
    10	# Never a key-only/size-only skip. Aggregates every worker exit; requires the EXACT
    11	# 10-tag inventory; fail-closed.
    12	#
    13	# 2026-08-07 (G-1): --bkg-mode is passed EXPLICITLY (from P4Config, not hardcoded) and
    14	# stamped into every receipt. The value is `purity` per the 2026-08-07 footing decision,
    15	# which is also the driver default -- so this changes provenance, not physics, and the
    16	# produced ROOTs must hash identically to the 2026-07-18 ones. If a re-unfold after this
    17	# change yields a different ROOT, STOP and find out why; do not adjust the manifest.
    18	set -o pipefail
    19	export HOME=/global/homes/j/josephrb
    20	REPO="/pscratch/sd/j/josephrb/MINERvA-OmniFold"; ND="${REPO}/nd-unfolding"

    25	CONC="${CONC:-4}"
    26	cd "${ND}"
    27	CFG_HASH=$(python3 -c "import p4_lib; c=p4_lib.P4Config(); c.validate(); print(c.hash())") || { echo "[p4-unfold] ABORT config"; exit 2; }
    28	# G-1 (2026-08-07): the background footing is passed EXPLICITLY, never inherited from the
    29	# driver default. Read from P4Config (validated above) rather than hardcoded here, so the
    30	# launcher and the manifest cannot drift apart. `purity` is also the driver default, so this
    31	# is a provenance change and a physics NO-OP: it must not move any output ROOT hash.
    32	BKG_MODE=$(python3 -c "import p4_lib; c=p4_lib.P4Config(); c.validate(); print(c.bkg_mode)") || { echo "[p4-unfold] ABORT bkg_mode"; exit 2; }

    34	echo "[p4-unfold] start $(date -u +%T) CONC=${CONC} config_hash=${CFG_HASH} bkg_mode=${BKG_MODE}"

    36	valid_root(){ python3 -c "import ROOT,sys; f=ROOT.TFile.Open('$1'); sys.exit(0 if (f and not f.IsZombie() and not f.TestBit(ROOT.TFile.kRecovered) and f.Get('hXSecND_flat') and f.Get('hXSecND_flat').GetNbinsX()==65856) else 1)" >/dev/null 2>&1; }
    37	attest(){ python3 -c "import json,hashlib,sys;m=json.load(open('$MANIFEST'));import p4_lib as P;sys.exit(0 if P.sha256_file('$1')==m['endpoint_sha256'].get('$2','') else 1)" >/dev/null 2>&1; }
    38	sha(){ python3 -c "import p4_lib;print(p4_lib.sha256_file('$1'))" 2>/dev/null; }

    40	unfold_one(){
    41	  local BAND="$1" EP="$2" tag="${1}_${2}"
    42	  local MERGED="${MERGEDIR}/runEventLoopOmniFold_5D_MEFHC_active_${tag}.root"
    43	  local OUT="${OUTDIR}/5d_xsec_MEFHC_5iter_lgbm_uni_full_${tag}.root"
    44	  local REC="${OUT}.done"
    45	  # D2a: the skip is now CONTENT-validating. A ROOT plus any nonempty .done used to be enough;
    46	  # p4_check_receipt.py re-derives root/central/config/bkg_mode identities live and compares the
    47	  # merged sha against the orchestrator receipt. A reject falls through and re-runs the endpoint.
    48	  if [[ -s "${OUT}" && -s "${REC}" ]] && valid_root "${OUT}"; then
    49	    if RCHK=$(python3 p4_check_receipt.py --receipt "${REC}" --tag "${tag}" \
    50	                --root "${OUT}" --merged "${MERGED}" 2>&1); then
    51	      echo "[unfold] SKIP ${tag} (receipt validated)"; return 0
    52	    fi
    53	    echo "[unfold] STALE ${tag} -> re-running: ${RCHK}"
    54	    rm -f "${REC}"                       # D2: never leave a stale ROOT/receipt pair behind
    55	  fi
    56	  if [[ -s "${OUT}" && ! -s "${REC}" ]] && valid_root "${OUT}" && [[ -f "${MANIFEST}" ]] && attest "${OUT}" "${tag}"; then
    57	    # D2b: the legacy receipt used to omit merged/central provenance, so an attested endpoint
    58	    # was permanently less provable than a produced one. It now carries the same fields.
    59	    local AMH ACH
    60	    AMH=$(python3 -c "import p4_check_receipt as C;print(C.committed_merged_sha('${MERGED}'))") || {
    61	      echo "[unfold] ABORT ${tag} cannot resolve committed merged sha"; return 6; }
    62	    ACH=$(sha "products/5d/xsec_5d_MEFHC_5iter_lgbm.root")
    63	    if ! { printf '{"tag":"%s","mode":"legacy-attested","root_sha256":"%s","merged_sha256":"%s","central5d_sha256":"%s","config_hash":"%s","bkg_mode":"%s","bkg_mode_basis":"log-branch-evidence (attestation certifies identity, not footing)","code_rev":"%s","t":"%s"}\n' \
    64	      "${tag}" "$(sha "${OUT}")" "${AMH}" "${ACH}" "${CFG_HASH}" "${BKG_MODE}" "${CODE_REV}" "$(date -u +%FT%TZ)" > "${REC}.tmp" && mv -f "${REC}.tmp" "${REC}"; }; then
    65	      echo "[unfold] FAIL ${tag} attest receipt publication failed"; rm -f "${REC}.tmp"; return 7
    66	    fi
    67	    echo "[unfold] ATTEST ${tag} (legacy ROOT sha256 == manifest)"; return 0
    68	  fi
    69	  [[ ! -s "${MERGED}" ]] && { echo "[unfold] ABORT ${tag} merged missing"; return 3; }
    70	  local TMP="${OUT}.$$.${RANDOM}.tmp.root"
    71	  rm -f "${TMP}"
    72	  if python3 unfold_nd_omnifold_unbinned.py --omnifile "${MERGED}" --axes eavail,q3,W \
    73	       --iters 5 --use-weights --estimator lgbm --seed 42 --bkg-mode "${BKG_MODE}" \
    74	       --out "${TMP}" --verbose \
    75	       > "${OUTDIR}/unfold_${tag}.log" 2>&1 && valid_root "${TMP}"; then
    76	    local MH CH RH; MH=$(sha "${MERGED}"); CH=$(sha "products/5d/xsec_5d_MEFHC_5iter_lgbm.root")
    77	    mv -f "${TMP}" "${OUT}"; RH=$(sha "${OUT}")                       # atomic ROOT publish
    78	    # D2c: this used to be an unchecked `printf … && mv`, so a failed receipt write still fell
    79	    # through to `echo DONE` and returned 0 -- a published ROOT with no receipt, reported as
    80	    # success. The write is now the function's success condition.
    81	    if ! { printf '{"tag":"%s","mode":"produced","root_sha256":"%s","merged_sha256":"%s","central5d_sha256":"%s","config_hash":"%s","bkg_mode":"%s","bkg_mode_basis":"passed explicitly to the driver by this launcher","code_rev":"%s","t":"%s"}\n' \
    82	      "${tag}" "${RH}" "${MH}" "${CH}" "${CFG_HASH}" "${BKG_MODE}" "${CODE_REV}" "$(date -u +%FT%TZ)" > "${REC}.tmp" && mv -f "${REC}.tmp" "${REC}"; }; then
    83	      echo "[unfold] FAIL ${tag} receipt publication failed after ROOT publish"; rm -f "${REC}.tmp"; return 8
    84	    fi
    85	    echo "[unfold] DONE ${tag}"
    86	  else
    87	    echo "[unfold] FAIL ${tag} (see unfold_${tag}.log)"; rm -f "${TMP}"; return 4
    88	  fi
    89	}
    90	
    91	declare -A RC
    92	for BAND in "${BANDS[@]}"; do for EP in 0 1; do
    93	  while [ "$(jobs -rp | wc -l)" -ge "$CONC" ]; do sleep 8; done
    94	  ( unfold_one "${BAND}" "${EP}" ) & RC["${BAND}_${EP}"]=$!
    95	done; done
    96	fail=0
    97	for tag in "${!RC[@]}"; do wait "${RC[$tag]}" || { echo "[p4-unfold] worker FAILED: ${tag}"; fail=1; }; done
    98

   100	missing=0
   101	for BAND in "${BANDS[@]}"; do for EP in 0 1; do
   102	  t="${BAND}_${EP}"; [[ -s "${OUTDIR}/5d_xsec_MEFHC_5iter_lgbm_uni_full_${t}.root" && -s "${OUTDIR}/5d_xsec_MEFHC_5iter_lgbm_uni_full_${t}.root.done" ]] || { echo "[p4-unfold] MISSING published ${t}"; missing=1; }
   103	done; done
   104	# D2d: the loop above only asks whether the ten EXPECTED tags exist, so an eleventh product in
   105	# the directory was invisible to it. Reject extras as well as missing -- an unexplained endpoint
   106	# in a publication namespace is exactly the state this lane spent three weeks in.

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	# STANDARD P4 stage-1: 10 endpoint hadd-merges (CONC-capped for Lustre) + acceptance
     3	# audit. Runs inside a held interactive CPU alloc (via alloc_run srun --overlap; the
     4	# whole node is ours, so bare-background hadds are fine). skip-if-exists.
     5	set -o pipefail
     6	export HOME=/global/homes/j/josephrb
     7	REPO="/pscratch/sd/j/josephrb/MINERvA-OmniFold"; ND="${REPO}/nd-unfolding"
     8	PLAYLISTS=(1A 1B 1C 1D 1E 1F 1G 1L 1M 1N 1O 1P)
     9	BANDS=(BeamAngleX BeamAngleY MuonResolution Muon_Energy_MINERvA Muon_Energy_MINOS)
    10	MERGEDIR="${ND}/active_universe_5d/standard/merged"; mkdir -p "${MERGEDIR}"
    11	CONC="${CONC:-4}"
    12	echo "[p4-merge] start $(date -u +%T) CONC=${CONC}"
    13	valid_merged () {  # a complete merged ROOT opens with the 4 trees
    14	  python3 -c "import ROOT,sys; f=ROOT.TFile.Open('$1'); sys.exit(0 if (f and not f.IsZombie() and f.Get('mc_truth_denom') and f.Get('mc_signal_reco') and f.Get('mc_background') and f.Get('data')) else 1)" >/dev/null 2>&1
    15	}
    16	merge_one () {
    17	  local BAND="$1" EP="$2"
    18	  local MERGED="${MERGEDIR}/runEventLoopOmniFold_5D_MEFHC_active_${BAND}_${EP}.root"
    19	  if [[ -s "${MERGED}" ]] && valid_merged "${MERGED}"; then echo "[merge] SKIP ${BAND}:${EP} valid ($(stat -c '%s' "${MERGED}")B)"; return 0; fi
    20	  [[ -s "${MERGED}" ]] && echo "[merge] REDO ${BAND}:${EP} (existing invalid/partial)" && rm -f "${MERGED}"
    21	  local INPUTS=(); for PL in "${PLAYLISTS[@]}"; do local f="${ND}/active_universe_5d/standard/${BAND}_${EP}/runEventLoopOmniFold_5D_${PL}_active_${BAND}_${EP}.root"; [[ -s "$f" ]] && INPUTS+=("$f"); done
    22	  [[ ${#INPUTS[@]} -ne 12 ]] && { echo "[merge] ABORT ${BAND}:${EP} ${#INPUTS[@]}/12"; return 3; }
    23	  python "${REPO}/2d-unfolding/uq/hadd_universes_full.py" "${MERGED}" "${INPUTS[@]}" > "${MERGEDIR}/merge_${BAND}_${EP}.log" 2>&1 \
    24	    && echo "[merge] DONE ${BAND}:${EP} ($(stat -c '%s' "${MERGED}")B)" || { echo "[merge] FAIL ${BAND}:${EP}"; rm -f "${MERGED}"; return 4; }
    25	}
    26	# J31 (AUDIT-FINDINGS-20260731): merge failures used to be converted into success, twice over.
    27	# `merge_one`'s last command on the failure path was `rm -f`, which returns 0 whether or not the
    28	# file exists, so the function exited 0; and a bare `wait` discards every child status anyway,
    29	# including the `return 3` abort the author did write. NMERGED was computed, printed, and never
    30	# compared to EXPECTED, and there is no `set -e`. Fixing only the `rm` would have changed nothing.
    31	# So: collect the PIDs, wait on each one individually, and fail closed on the count.
    32	EXPECTED=$(( ${#BANDS[@]} * 2 ))
    33	PIDS=(); LABELS=()
    34	for BAND in "${BANDS[@]}"; do for EP in 0 1; do
    35	  while [ "$(jobs -rp | wc -l)" -ge "$CONC" ]; do sleep 5; done
    36	  merge_one "$BAND" "$EP" &
    37	  PIDS+=($!); LABELS+=("${BAND}:${EP}")
    38	done; done
    39	NFAILED=0
    40	for i in "${!PIDS[@]}"; do
    41	  if ! wait "${PIDS[$i]}"; then
    42	    echo "[merge] child ${LABELS[$i]} exited non-zero (rc=$?)" >&2
    43	    NFAILED=$(( NFAILED + 1 ))
    44	  fi
    45	done
    46	NMERGED=$(find "${MERGEDIR}" -name 'runEventLoopOmniFold_5D_MEFHC_active_*.root' -size +0c 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
    47	echo "[p4-merge] merges done $(date -u +%T); merged=${NMERGED}/${EXPECTED}; failed_children=${NFAILED}"
    48	if [[ "${NFAILED}" -ne 0 || "${NMERGED}" -ne "${EXPECTED}" ]]; then
    49	  echo "[p4-merge][FAIL] ${NMERGED}/${EXPECTED} merged, ${NFAILED} child failure(s) -- refusing to" >&2
    50	  echo "  run the acceptance audit on an incomplete merge set. Re-run; merge_one is idempotent" >&2
    51	  echo "  (it SKIPs a valid merged file and REDOes an invalid one)." >&2
    52	  exit 5
    53	fi

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Endpoint-receipt gate for run_p4_unfold_std.sh's resume path (repair-4, verifier defect 2).
     3	
     4	Exit 0 iff the receipt is complete and every recorded identity matches the live/committed one;
     5	otherwise print `RECEIPT-REJECT :: <reason>` and exit 1, which makes the launcher re-run the
     6	endpoint transactionally instead of skipping it.
     7	
     8	Deliberately a separate CLI rather than inline `python3 -c` in the launcher: the previous skip
     9	logic was an unreadable one-liner, and a gate that cannot be unit-tested is the defect this
    10	whole repair round exists to remove. ROOT-free -- content validity of the ROOT itself is the
    11	launcher's `valid_root`, this is the provenance half.
    12	
    13	Usage:
    14	  p4_check_receipt.py --receipt R.done --tag BAND_EP --root OUT.root --merged MERGED.root
    15	"""
    16	import argparse, json, os, sys
    17	
    18	sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
    19	import p4_lib as P
    20	
    21	REPO = "/pscratch/sd/j/josephrb/MINERvA-OmniFold"
    22	ND = f"{REPO}/nd-unfolding"
    23	CEN5 = f"{ND}/products/5d/xsec_5d_MEFHC_5iter_lgbm.root"
    24	RECDIR = f"{REPO}/docs/orchestration/state/merged-input-hashes/p4-merged-20260718"
    25	
    26	
    27	def committed_merged_sha(merged_path):
    28	    """The orchestrator receipt's committed hash for this merged file, after its own live
    29	    size+integer-mtime check. Never re-hashes 53.8 GB."""
    30	    live = {}
    31	    for b in P.BANDS:
    32	        for ep in P.ENDPOINTS:
    33	            rel = (f"nd-unfolding/active_universe_5d/standard/merged/"
    34	                   f"runEventLoopOmniFold_5D_MEFHC_active_{b}_{ep}.root")
    35	            ap = f"{REPO}/{rel}"
    36	            st = os.stat(ap)
    37	            live[rel] = (st.st_size, int(st.st_mtime))
    38	    rec = P.validate_orchestrator_merged_receipt(RECDIR, live)
    39	    base = os.path.basename(merged_path)
    40	    for p, h in rec["merged_sha256"].items():
    41	        if os.path.basename(p) == base:
    42	            return h
    43	    raise P.P4GateError(f"{base} not in the orchestrator merged receipt")
    44	
    45	
    46	def main():
    47	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    48	    ap.add_argument("--receipt", required=True)
    49	    ap.add_argument("--tag", required=True)
    50	    ap.add_argument("--root", required=True)
    51	    ap.add_argument("--merged", required=True)
    52	    a = ap.parse_args()
    53	    try:
    54	        if not os.path.exists(a.receipt) or os.path.getsize(a.receipt) == 0:
    55	            raise P.P4GateError("receipt absent or empty")
    56	        try:
    57	            rec = json.load(open(a.receipt))
    58	        except Exception as e:
    59	            raise P.P4GateError(f"receipt is not valid JSON ({e})")
    60	        cfg = P.P4Config(); cfg.validate()
    61	        P.validate_endpoint_receipt(
    62	            rec, tag=a.tag,
    63	            root_sha256=P.sha256_file(a.root),
    64	            merged_sha256=committed_merged_sha(a.merged),
    65	            central5d_sha256=P.sha256_file(CEN5),
    66	            config_hash=cfg.hash(),
    67	            bkg_mode=cfg.bkg_mode)
    68	    except P.P4GateError as e:
    69	        print(f"RECEIPT-REJECT :: {e}"); sys.exit(1)
    70	    except Exception as e:                      # never let an unexpected error read as PASS
    71	        print(f"RECEIPT-REJECT :: unexpected {type(e).__name__}: {e}"); sys.exit(1)
    72	    print(f"RECEIPT-OK :: {a.tag}")
    73	    sys.exit(0)
    74

    76	if __name__ == "__main__":
    77	    main()

   283	def validate_endpoint_receipt(rec, *, tag, root_sha256, merged_sha256,
   284	                              central5d_sha256, config_hash, bkg_mode):
   285	    """Fail closed unless the receipt is complete AND every recorded identity matches the
   286	    live/committed one. Returns True, or raises P4GateError naming the first mismatch."""
   287	    require(isinstance(rec, dict) and rec, f"receipt for {tag} is not a JSON object")
   288	    missing = [k for k in RECEIPT_REQUIRED_KEYS if k not in rec]
   289	    require(not missing, f"receipt {tag} missing required keys {missing} (incomplete legacy format)")
   290	    require(rec["mode"] in RECEIPT_MODES, f"receipt {tag} unknown mode {rec['mode']!r}")
   291	    require(rec["tag"] == tag, f"receipt tag {rec['tag']!r} != {tag!r} (receipt/ROOT pair mismatch)")
   292	    require(rec["root_sha256"] == root_sha256,
   293	            f"receipt {tag} root_sha256 drift: recorded {rec['root_sha256']} vs live {root_sha256}")
   294	    require(rec["merged_sha256"] == merged_sha256,
   295	            f"receipt {tag} merged_sha256 drift vs the orchestrator receipt")
   296	    require(rec["central5d_sha256"] == central5d_sha256,
   297	            f"receipt {tag} central5d_sha256 drift (the central moved under this endpoint)")
   298	    require(rec["config_hash"] == config_hash,
   299	            f"receipt {tag} config_hash drift: produced under a different unfold configuration")
   300	    require(rec["bkg_mode"] == bkg_mode,
   301	            f"receipt {tag} bkg_mode {rec['bkg_mode']!r} != declared {bkg_mode!r}")
   302	    require(isinstance(rec["code_rev"], str) and rec["code_rev"],
   303	            f"receipt {tag} has no code_rev")
   304	    return True

   306	
   307	def cmask_order_hash_4d(mask):
   308	    """4D reported-mask + C-order fingerprint (repair-4, defect 5c).
   309	
   310	    `mask_order_hash` fails closed on GRID_NBINS, which is the 5D grid, so the 4D mask had no
   311	    hash helper and the projector compared only the reported COUNT. A count is not an ordering:
   312	    a permuted 4D mask with the same population passed. Same construction as the 5D helper --
   313	    C-order indices of the selected bins -- so the two are comparable by eye in a manifest.
   314	    Deliberately NOT size-pinned: the 4D grid is a marginal of the 5D one and the standard lane
   315	    has no pinned canonical 4D target (see RUNBOOK-20260807 §7.4)."""
   316	    idx = np.nonzero(np.asarray(mask))[0].astype(np.int64)
   317	    require(idx.size > 0, "4D reported mask is empty")
   318	    return hashlib.sha256(idx.tobytes() + b"|C").hexdigest()
   319	
   320	
   321	def matrix_content_hash(M):
   322	    """Deterministic digest of a matrix's CONTENTS (repair-4, defect 5d).
   323	
   324	    The projection receipt recorded only `M_shape`, which two different projectors of the same
   325	    dimensions share. Hashes C-contiguous float64 bytes plus the shape, so a changed weight is
   326	    caught and a transposed/reshaped M cannot collide with the original."""
   327	    A = np.ascontiguousarray(np.asarray(M, dtype=np.float64))
   328	    h = hashlib.sha256()
   329	    h.update(repr(A.shape).encode()); h.update(b"|C|f8|"); h.update(A.tobytes())
   330	    return h.hexdigest()
   331	
   332	
   333	def require_exact_endpoint_tags(tags):
   334	    """Reject BOTH missing and EXTRA tags. The old inventory only looked for the ten expected
   335	    names, so an eleventh product in the directory was invisible to it."""
   336	    got, want = set(tags), {f"{b}_{e}" for b in BANDS for e in ENDPOINTS}
   337	    require(not (want - got), f"missing endpoint tags: {sorted(want - got)}")
   338	    require(not (got - want), f"unexpected extra endpoint tags: {sorted(got - want)}")
   339	    return True
   340	
   341	
   342	def sha256_file(path, _bufsz=1 << 20):
   343	    """Durable file digest (login-computable; no ROOT)."""
   344	    h = hashlib.sha256()
   345	    with open(path, "rb") as fh:
   346	        for chunk in iter(lambda: fh.read(_bufsz), b""):
   347	            h.update(chunk)
   348	    return h.hexdigest()
   349	
   350	
   351	def canonical_endpoints():
   352	    """The exact 10 (band, endpoint) logical tasks, in fixed order."""
   353	    return [(b, ep) for b in BANDS for ep in ENDPOINTS]
   354	
   355	
   356	def endpoint_manifest_hash(entries):
   357	    """entries: iterable of (band, endpoint, fingerprint). Hash is order-independent
   358	    over the SET but requires the exact 10-endpoint inventory (fail-closed)."""
   359	    seen = {}
   360	    for band, ep, fp in entries:
   361	        require(band in BANDS, f"unknown band {band!r}")
   362	        require(ep in ENDPOINTS, f"bad endpoint {ep!r}")
   363	        seen[(band, int(ep))] = str(fp)
   364	    require(len(seen) == N_ENDPOINTS,
   365	            f"expected exactly {N_ENDPOINTS} endpoints, got {len(seen)} "
   366	            f"(missing {sorted(set(canonical_endpoints())-set(seen))})")
   367	    blob = json.dumps({f"{b}_{e}": seen[(b, e)] for (b, e) in canonical_endpoints()},
   368	                      sort_keys=True)
   369	    return hashlib.sha256(blob.encode()).hexdigest()
   370	
   371	
   372	def mask_order_hash(mask):
   373	    """Hash of the reported-bin mask + C-order. mask: 1-D bool/int array over the
   374	    full grid. Pins which bins are reported and their order so a projection or a
   375	    component sum can never silently mix masks."""
   376	    m = np.asarray(mask)
   377	    require(m.ndim == 1, "mask must be 1-D (C-order ravel over the 5D grid)")
   378	    require(m.size == GRID_NBINS, f"mask size {m.size} != grid {GRID_NBINS}")
   379	    idx = np.nonzero(m.astype(bool))[0].astype(np.int64)
   380	    require(idx.size > 0, "mask selects zero reported bins")
   381	    return hashlib.sha256(idx.tobytes() + b"|C").hexdigest(), int(idx.size)
   382	
   383	
   384	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- inventory gates
   385	def require_complete_unfold_set(present_tags):
   386	    """present_tags: iterable of '<band>_<ep>' that passed CONTENT validation.
   387	    Fail-closed unless all 10 canonical endpoints are present+valid."""
   388	    present = set(present_tags)
   389	    need = {f"{b}_{e}" for (b, e) in canonical_endpoints()}
   390	    missing = sorted(need - present)
   391	    require(not missing, f"incomplete unfold set: missing/invalid {missing}")
   392	    require(present == need, f"unexpected unfold tags: {sorted(present - need)}")
   393	    return True
   394	
   395	
   396	def check_merged_metadata(meta):
   397	    """Merged-endpoint audit on extracted metadata (ROOT-free, so testable).
   398	    meta keys: tree_entries{mc_truth_denom,mc_signal_reco,mc_background,data},
   399	    mcPOT, dataPOT, hasTruthOnlyMisses, nTruthOnlyMisses,
   400	    census{TruthEntrants,TruthExits,RecoEntrants,RecoExits}, migration_policy."""
   401	    te = meta.get("tree_entries", {})
   402	    for t in ("mc_truth_denom", "mc_signal_reco", "mc_background", "data"):
   403	        require(te.get(t, 0) and te[t] > 0, f"merged tree {t} empty/absent")
   404	    mc, da = meta.get("mcPOT"), meta.get("dataPOT")
   405	    require(mc is not None and np.isfinite(mc) and mc > 0, "merged mcPOT not finite-positive")
   406	    require(da is not None and np.isfinite(da) and da > 0, "merged dataPOT not finite-positive")
   407	    require(te["mc_signal_reco"] == te["mc_truth_denom"],
   408	            f"signal_reco {te['mc_signal_reco']} != truth_denom {te['mc_truth_denom']} (completeness broken)")
   409	    require(meta.get("hasTruthOnlyMisses") is not None and meta.get("nTruthOnlyMisses") is not None,
   410	            "native-miss metadata missing")
   411	    cen = meta.get("census", {})
   412	    for k in ("TruthEntrants", "TruthExits", "RecoEntrants", "RecoExits"):
   413	        require(k in cen and cen[k] is not None, f"census counter {k} missing")
   414	    require(meta.get("migration_policy"), "declared migration policy missing")
   415	    return True
   416	
   417	
   418	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- covariance gates
   419	def check_symmetric_psd(C, rtol_sym=1e-9, psd_atol_ratio=1e-12):
   420	    C = np.asarray(C, dtype=float)
   421	    require(C.ndim == 2 and C.shape[0] == C.shape[1], "covariance must be square 2-D")
   422	    require(np.all(np.isfinite(C)), "covariance has non-finite entries")
   423	    denom = max(1e-300, np.max(np.abs(C)))
   424	    asym = np.max(np.abs(C - C.T)) / denom
   425	    require(asym <= rtol_sym, f"covariance not symmetric (rel {asym:.2e})")
   426	    ev = np.linalg.eigvalsh(0.5 * (C + C.T))
   427	    require(ev[0] >= -psd_atol_ratio * abs(ev[-1]),
   428	            f"covariance not PSD (min/max eig {ev[0]/max(1e-300,abs(ev[-1])):.2e})")
   429	    d = np.diag(C)
   430	    require(np.all(np.isfinite(d)) and np.all(d >= -1e-30), "non-finite/negative diagonal")
   431	    return {"rel_asymmetry": float(asym), "min_eig": float(ev[0]), "max_eig": float(ev[-1])}
   432	
   433	
   434	def require_exact_bands(band_covs):
   435	    """band_covs: dict band -> cov. Must be EXACTLY the 5 kinematic bands."""
   436	    got = set(band_covs)
   437	    require(got == set(BANDS),
   438	            f"active-lateral bands mismatch: got {sorted(got)}, need {sorted(BANDS)}")
   439	    return True
   440	
   441	
   442	def component_traces_positive_finite(band_covs):
   443	    tr = {}
   444	    for b in BANDS:
   445	        require(b in band_covs, f"missing component band {b}")
   446	        C = np.asarray(band_covs[b], dtype=float)
   447	        require(np.all(np.isfinite(C)), f"component {b} has non-finite entries")
   448	        t = float(np.trace(C))
   449	        require(np.isfinite(t) and t > 0.0, f"component {b} trace not positive-finite ({t})")
   450	        tr[b] = t
   451	    return tr
   452	
   453	
   454	def check_component_sum(total, band_covs, rtol=1e-9):
   455	    """total must equal the EXACT sum of the 5 per-band component covariances."""
   456	    require_exact_bands(band_covs)
   457	    total = np.asarray(total, dtype=float)
   458	    s = np.zeros_like(total)
   459	    for b in BANDS:
   460	        Cb = np.asarray(band_covs[b], dtype=float)
   461	        require(Cb.shape == total.shape, f"component {b} shape {Cb.shape} != total {total.shape}")
   462	        s = s + Cb
   463	    denom = max(1e-300, np.max(np.abs(total)))
   464	    err = np.max(np.abs(total - s)) / denom
   465	    require(err <= rtol, f"component sum mismatch (rel {err:.2e}) — total != sum of bands")
   466	    return float(err)
   467	
   468	
   469	def check_support_comparison(active_cov, support_cov):
   470	    """Complete support comparison: both present, same shape/mask/order. Returns
   471	    sqrt-trace ratio (active/support). Fail-closed if the support block is absent."""
   472	    require(active_cov is not None, "active lateral block missing")
   473	    require(support_cov is not None, "support-limited comparison block missing")
   474	    A = np.asarray(active_cov, dtype=float); S = np.asarray(support_cov, dtype=float)
   475	    require(A.shape == S.shape, f"active/support shape mismatch {A.shape} vs {S.shape}")
   476	    sta = float(np.sqrt(max(0.0, np.trace(A)))); sts = float(np.sqrt(max(0.0, np.trace(S))))
   477	    require(sts > 0, "support block has zero trace")
   478	    return {"sqrt_tr_active": sta, "sqrt_tr_support": sts, "ratio": sta / sts}
   479	
   480	
   481	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- deterministic projection map
   482	def build_projection_M(edges, drop_axis, mask_high, mask_low):
   483	    """Deterministic 5D->4D map by WIDTH-WEIGHTED marginalization of one axis.
   484	    edges: ordered per-axis edge arrays (pt,pz,eavail,q3,W). drop_axis: axis index to
   485	    marginalize (W=4). mask_high/mask_low: reported-bin bool masks over the full high/low
   486	    grids (C-order). Returns dense M (n_low_reported x n_high_reported) with
   487	    M[a,b] = width_drop[k] when high bin b decomposes to low bin a + dropped index k.
   488	    density convention: x_low = M @ x_high, C_low = M C_high M^T."""
   489	    nb = [np.asarray(e).size - 1 for e in edges]
   490	    require(len(nb) == 5, "expected 5 axes")
   491	    total_high = int(np.prod(nb))
   492	    require(int(np.asarray(mask_high).size) == total_high, "mask_high size != high grid")
   493	    wdrop = np.asarray(edges[drop_axis], float)[1:] - np.asarray(edges[drop_axis], float)[:-1]
   494	    nb_low = [n for i, n in enumerate(nb) if i != drop_axis]
   495	    total_low = int(np.prod(nb_low))
   496	    require(int(np.asarray(mask_low).size) == total_low, "mask_low size != low grid")
   497	    strides_h = np.array([int(np.prod(nb[i + 1:])) for i in range(5)])          # C-order strides
   498	    strides_l = np.array([int(np.prod(nb_low[i + 1:])) for i in range(4)])
   499	    mh = np.nonzero(np.asarray(mask_high).astype(bool))[0]
   500	    ml = np.nonzero(np.asarray(mask_low).astype(bool))[0]
   501	    low_pos = {int(g): r for r, g in enumerate(ml)}                              # low global -> reported row
   502	    M = np.zeros((ml.size, mh.size), dtype=float)
   503	    for col, g in enumerate(mh):
   504	        midx = [(g // strides_h[i]) % nb[i] for i in range(5)]                   # 5D multi-index
   505	        k = midx[drop_axis]
   506	        low_multi = [midx[i] for i in range(5) if i != drop_axis]
   507	        glow = int(np.dot(low_multi, strides_l))
   508	        row = low_pos.get(glow)
   509	        require(row is not None, f"high reported bin {g} maps to non-reported low bin {glow}")
   510	        M[row, col] = wdrop[k]
   511	    return M
   512	
   513	
   514	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- projection gates
   515	def project(C_high, M):
   516	    """5D->4D (or any) projection C_low = M C_high M^T (preserves density/order)."""
   517	    C_high = np.asarray(C_high, dtype=float); M = np.asarray(M, dtype=float)
   518	    require(M.shape[1] == C_high.shape[0], f"M cols {M.shape[1]} != C dim {C_high.shape[0]}")
   519	    return M @ C_high @ M.T
   520	
   521	
   522	def check_projection_nonmutation(C_high, M, x_high, x_low_frozen, rtol_central=3e-2):
   523	    """Enforce projected covariance validity AND central non-mutation:
   524	    C_low = M C_high M^T is symmetric/PSD, and M @ x_high reproduces the frozen
   525	    lower-dim central within tolerance (the projection must not mutate the central)."""
   526	    C_low = project(C_high, M)
   527	    stats = check_symmetric_psd(C_low)
   528	    xin = np.asarray(x_high, dtype=float); xfr = np.asarray(x_low_frozen, dtype=float)
   529	    proj = M @ xin
   530	    require(proj.shape == xfr.shape, f"projected central shape {proj.shape} != frozen {xfr.shape}")
   531	    denom = np.where(np.abs(xfr) > 0, np.abs(xfr), 1.0)
   532	    rel = float(np.max(np.abs(proj - xfr) / denom))
   533	    require(rel <= rtol_central, f"projection mutates central (max rel {rel:.2e})")
   534	    stats["central_max_rel"] = rel
   535	    return C_low, stats

    75	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- config / hashes
    76	class P4Config:
    77	    """Frozen unfold configuration; its hash pins seed/axes/iters/estimator so a
    78	    covariance can never be built from mismatched-config endpoints."""
    79	    def __init__(self, axes="eavail,q3,W", iters=5, seed=42, estimator="lgbm",
    80	                 use_weights=True, universe=None, bkg_mode=STANDARD_BKG_MODE):
    81	        self.axes = axes; self.iters = int(iters); self.seed = int(seed)
    82	        self.estimator = estimator; self.use_weights = bool(use_weights)
    83	        self.universe = universe          # MUST be None for active endpoints
    84	        self.bkg_mode = bkg_mode          # G-1: never inherited from the driver default
    85	    def as_dict(self):
    86	        # repair-4 (D3a): `full_phase_space_reported_grid` used to be bolted onto
    87	        # man["config"] in p4_evidence.py AFTER config_hash was computed, so the recorded hash
    88	        # did not cover the recorded configuration -- the declared config was not hash-bound.

    91	                "estimator": self.estimator, "use_weights": self.use_weights,

    93	                "full_phase_space_reported_grid": GRID_NBINS}
    94	    def hash(self):
    95	        return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(self.as_dict(), sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()
    96	    def footing(self):
    97	        """The nested footing block as the PRODUCER emits it. Consumers must read this
    98	        shape, not a flattened copy of it -- a fixture shaped like the consumer is how
    99	        BEN-040's gate stayed green while failing on every real input."""
   100	        f = dict(STANDARD_REQUIRED_FOOTING)
   101	        f.update({"estimator": self.estimator, "seed": self.seed, "iters": self.iters,
   102	                  "use_weights": self.use_weights, "bkg_mode": self.bkg_mode})
   103	        return f

   106	        require(self.seed == 42, f"standard P4 requires fixed seed 42 (got {self.seed})")
   107	        require(self.use_weights, "standard P4 requires --use-weights")
   108	        require(self.iters == 5, f"standard P4 production uses 5 iters (got {self.iters})")
   109	        require(self.axes == "eavail,q3,W", f"standard P4 requires axes=eavail,q3,W (got {self.axes})")
   110	        require(self.estimator == "lgbm", f"standard P4 requires estimator=lgbm (got {self.estimator})")

   120	# ------------------------------------------------------- footing evidence from logs (G-1)
   121	# The driver announces its background mode on the NEGWEIGHT branches only
   122	# (unfold_nd_omnifold_unbinned.py:842,895 -> "[INFO] bkg-mode=<mode>: ..."); the purity
   123	# branch (:883) prints nothing about mode and instead calls build_measured_training_nd,
   124	# whose verbose line is "[INFO] measured training: sum=... zero=.../...".
   125	#
   126	# So in this two-branch print, SILENCE IS A BRANCH: a log with the measured-training line
   127	# and no bkg-mode line positively identifies purity rather than leaving it unknown. That is
   128	# exactly the inference BEN-041 records failing to make. Both the flag and both
   129	# announcements landed together in cf8a4a6 (2026-07-11), before the 2026-07-18 endpoint
   130	# ROOTs, so an absent announcement is informative and not a version gap.
   131	_BKG_MODE_ANNOUNCE = "[INFO] bkg-mode="
   132	_PURITY_SIGNATURE = "[INFO] measured training:"
   133	
   134	
   135	def classify_log_bkg_mode(text):
   136	    """Return (mode, reason) for one unfold log. mode is a member of KNOWN_BKG_MODES, or
   137	    None when the log cannot decide -- which callers must treat as UNPROVABLE, not as
   138	    'probably fine'."""
   139	    announced = None
   140	    for line in text.splitlines():
   141	        i = line.find(_BKG_MODE_ANNOUNCE)
   142	        if i >= 0:
   143	            tail = line[i + len(_BKG_MODE_ANNOUNCE):]
   144	            tok = tail.split(":", 1)[0].strip()
   145	            if tok in KNOWN_BKG_MODES:
   146	                if announced is not None and announced != tok:
   147	                    return None, f"log announces conflicting modes {announced!r} and {tok!r}"
   148	                announced = tok
   149	            else:
   150	                return None, f"log announces unrecognized bkg-mode {tok!r}"
   151	    if announced is not None:
   152	        if announced == "purity":
   153	            # the purity branch never announces; an announcement claiming purity means the
   154	            # log did not come from this driver's known print set.
   155	            return None, "log announces bkg-mode=purity, which this driver's purity branch never prints"
   156	        return announced, f"driver announced bkg-mode={announced}"
   157	    if _PURITY_SIGNATURE in text:
   158	        return "purity", ("no bkg-mode announcement + build_measured_training_nd signature "
   159	                          "present => purity branch (the silent branch)")
   160	    return None, ("no bkg-mode announcement and no measured-training signature; the run may "
   161	                  "have been non-verbose, so the branch is unprovable from this log")

   165	    """Fail closed unless the manifest carries a complete footing block that matches the
   166	    standard requirement. Mirrors fps_provenance.require_footing's semantics -- absent is
   167	    'unprovable' and fails, exactly like mismatched -- without importing or mutating it."""
   168	    foot = manifest.get("footing")

   173	        require(foot.get(k) == v, f"footing.{k}={foot.get(k)!r} != {v!r}")

   179	                f"footing.bkg_mode={foot['bkg_mode']!r} != required {required_bkg_mode!r}")
   180	    return True

   190	def require_candidate_path(path):
   191	    """Positive allowlist + negative denylist: a candidate MUST live under the
   192	    candidate subdir and MUST NOT match any adopted/protected token. Prevents both
   193	    the round-2 self-rejection (candidate name containing '_final') and any write
   194	    onto an adopted/central path."""
   195	    # repair-4 (defect 4e): this was a bare substring test, so any path merely CONTAINING the
   196	    # candidate directory passed -- including one that traverses back out of it, e.g.
   197	    # ".../standard/candidate/../../../products/5d/x.root". Normalize first (which collapses
   198	    # every "..") and then require the candidate directory to appear as a contiguous run of
   199	    # real path COMPONENTS, so containment is structural rather than textual.
   200	    norm = os.path.normpath(path)
   201	    require(".." not in norm.split(os.sep),
   202	            f"candidate path escapes via '..' after normalization (got {path} -> {norm})")
   203	    parts = norm.split(os.sep)
   204	    want = CANDIDATE_SUBDIR.split("/")
   205	    contained = any(parts[i:i + len(want)] == want for i in range(len(parts) - len(want) + 1))
   206	    require(contained, f"candidate must be under {CANDIDATE_SUBDIR} (got {path})")
   207	    for t in _ADOPTED_TOKENS:
   208	        require(t not in path, f"refusing candidate onto adopted/protected path (token {t!r})")
   209	    return True

   212	def prove_identity(A, B, rtol, label):
   213	    """Fail-closed max-relative-difference identity gate (no subtraction hidden)."""
   214	    A = np.asarray(A, dtype=float); B = np.asarray(B, dtype=float)
   215	    require(A.shape == B.shape, f"{label}: shape {A.shape} != {B.shape}")
   216	    denom = max(1e-300, float(np.max(np.abs(A))))
   217	    err = float(np.max(np.abs(A - B)) / denom)
   218	    require(err <= rtol, f"{label}: identity broken (rel {err:.2e} > {rtol:.0e})")
   219	    return err

   221	
   222	def edges_bin_volume_hash(edges):
   223	    """Hash the ordered 5-axis edge arrays + the C-order per-bin volume vector."""
   224	    import numpy as _np
   225	    parts = []
   226	    for e in edges:
   227	        a = _np.asarray(e, dtype=float); require(a.ndim == 1 and a.size >= 2, "bad edge array")
   228	        parts.append(a.tobytes())
   229	    edge_hash = hashlib.sha256(b"|".join(parts)).hexdigest()
   230	    widths = [(_np.asarray(e, float)[1:] - _np.asarray(e, float)[:-1]) for e in edges]
   231	    vol = widths[0]
   232	    for w in widths[1:]:
   233	        vol = _np.multiply.outer(vol, w).ravel()   # C-order product of bin widths
   234	    vol_hash = hashlib.sha256(vol.astype(float).tobytes() + b"|C").hexdigest()
   235	    return {"edge_hash": edge_hash, "bin_volume_hash": vol_hash, "n_bins": int(vol.size)}

   238	def validate_orchestrator_merged_receipt(recdir, live_stat):
   239	    """Consume the owner-neutral merged-hash receipt: require COMPLETE + the four
   240	    files, recompute live size/integer-mtime vs the inventory, and return the
   241	    committed hash-list digest bound into our manifest. live_stat: dict path->(size,mtime)."""
   242	    import os as _os
   243	    for f in ("COMPLETE", "summary.tsv", "validation.tsv", "standard.sha256", "standard.inventory.tsv"):
   244	        require(_os.path.exists(_os.path.join(recdir, f)), f"orchestrator receipt missing {f}")
   245	    sha_lines = [l for l in open(_os.path.join(recdir, "standard.sha256")).read().splitlines() if l.strip()]
   246	    require(len(sha_lines) == N_ENDPOINTS, f"receipt standard.sha256 has {len(sha_lines)} != 10 lines")
   247	    merged = {}
   248	    for l in sha_lines:
   249	        h, p = l.split(None, 1); merged[p.strip()] = h
   250	    # recompute live size + integer mtime against the committed inventory
   251	    inv = {}
   252	    for l in open(_os.path.join(recdir, "standard.inventory.tsv")).read().splitlines():
   253	        if not l.strip() or l.startswith("#"):
   254	            continue
   255	        cols = l.split("\t")
   256	        if len(cols) >= 3:                       # orchestrator format: size<TAB>mtime<TAB>path
   257	            inv[cols[2]] = (int(cols[0]), int(float(cols[1])))
   258	    for p, (sz, mt) in inv.items():
   259	        require(p in live_stat, f"inventory path not live: {p}")
   260	        lsz, lmt = live_stat[p]
   261	        require(lsz == sz and int(lmt) == mt, f"live size/mtime drift for {p}")
   262	    digest = hashlib.sha256("\n".join(sorted(f"{merged[p]}  {p}" for p in merged)).encode()).hexdigest()
   263	    return {"merged_sha256": merged, "hash_list_digest": digest, "n": len(merged)}
